# Per-entry classification of every changed Showcase visual digest

Recorded baseline: `crates/showcase-ui/tests/baselines/showcase.txt` at the coordinator head `7bdf030be`.

Before-frame provenance. The recorded file is a mixture of several eras. For every key, the era whose tree reproduces the recorded digest exactly is named in the `origin` column:

* `origin` — the origin era `43016c3cb`, whose tree reproduces the recorded digest (680 of the 704 live keys are covered by one of the three eras).
* `held` — the held era `7bb9f2353`.
* `era` — the baseline era `e830877cd`.

The `cells` column is the cell count that moved away from each foreground colour value between the before-frame and the head frame. A cell count of `0` means the value is present in both frames; a positive or negative count is the net number of cells that changed to or from it.
